-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// The matrix is distributed by cyclic row blocks.  Keeping a complete row on
+// its owner makes the trailing update contiguous in memory, while cycling the
+// blocks balances the progressively larger updates near the bottom of L.
+
+size_t chooseBlockSize(const size_t n, const int ranks) {
+    constexpr size_t minBlockSize = 16;
+    constexpr size_t maxBlockSize = 128;
+    const size_t target = (n + 4 * static_cast<size_t>(ranks) - 1) /
+                          (4 * static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
+    return std::min(n, std::clamp(target, minBlockSize, maxBlockSize));
+}
+
+std::vector<size_t> makeLocalRows(const size_t n, const size_t blockSize,
+                                  const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    std::vector<size_t> rows;
+    const size_t blocks = (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize;
+
+    for (size_t block = static_cast<size_t>(rank); block < blocks;
+         block += static_cast<size_t>(ranks)) {
+        const size_t first = block * blockSize;
+        const size_t last = std::min(n, first + blockSize);
+        for (size_t row = first; row < last; ++row) {
+            rows.push_back(row);
+        }
+    }
+    return rows;
+}
+
+std::vector<size_t> makeRowsPerRank(const size_t n, const size_t blockSize,
+                                    const int ranks) {
+    std::vector<size_t> rowCounts(ranks, 0);
+    const size_t blocks = (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize;
+
+    for (size_t block = 0; block < blocks; ++block) {
+        const size_t first = block * blockSize;
+        rowCounts[block % static_cast<size_t>(ranks)] +=
+            std::min(n, first + blockSize) - first;
+    }
+    return rowCounts;
+}
+
+int mpiCount(const size_t count) {
+    // Matrices large enough to exceed an MPI int count require more memory
+    // than this benchmark can reasonably allocate on a single rank.
+    return static_cast<int>(count);
+}
+
+void broadcastDoubles(std::vector<double>& data, const int root,
+                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < data.size(); offset += maxCount) {
+        const size_t count = std::min(maxCount, data.size() - offset);
+        MPI_Bcast(data.data() + offset, mpiCount(count), MPI_DOUBLE, root,
+                  communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+void generateRandomRows(std::vector<double>& rows, unsigned int& seed) {
+    for (double& value : rows) {
+        value = (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 0.5;
+    }
+}
+
+void generateDistributedPositiveDefiniteMatrix(
+    std::vector<double>& localMatrix, const std::vector<size_t>& localRows,
+    const std::vector<size_t>& localIndexByGlobalRow, const size_t n,
+    const size_t blockSize, const int rank) {
+    // Retain only the random rows owned by this rank.  Broadcasting the source
+    // matrix twice (once to distribute rows and once to form B * B^T) avoids a
+    // full n-by-n temporary on every rank and keeps memory scalable.
+    std::vector<double> localB(localRows.size() * n);
+    std::vector<double> randomRows;
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+
+    for (size_t firstRow = 0; firstRow < n; firstRow += blockSize) {
+        const size_t rowCount = std::min(blockSize, n - firstRow);
+        randomRows.resize(rowCount * n);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            generateRandomRows(randomRows, seed);
+        }
+        broadcastDoubles(randomRows, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+            }
+        }

+

+    seed = 42;

+        const size_t rowCount = std::min(blockSize, n - firstRow);
+        randomRows.resize(rowCount * n);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            generateRandomRows(randomRows, seed);
+        }
+        broadcastDoubles(randomRows, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < localRows.size(); ++localRow) {
+            const double* const bRow = localB.data() + localRow * n;
+            double* const aRow = localMatrix.data() + localRow * n + firstRow;
+            for (size_t row = 0; row < rowCount; ++row) {
+                const double* const bColumnRow = randomRows.data() + row * n;
+                double sum = 0.0;

+            }
+        }
+    }

+        localMatrix[localRow * n + localRows[localRow]] +=
+            static_cast<double>(n);
+    }
+}

+void clearLocalUpperTriangle(std::vector<double>& localMatrix,
+                             const std::vector<size_t>& localRows,
+                             const size_t n) {
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < localRows.size(); ++localRow) {
+        double* const row = localMatrix.data() + localRow * n;
+        std::fill(row + localRows[localRow] + 1, row + n, 0.0);
+    }
+}

+bool factorDiagonalBlock(std::vector<double>& localMatrix,

+void solveLocalPanelRows(std::vector<double>& localMatrix,
+                         const std::vector<size_t>& localRows,
+                         const size_t n, const size_t panelStart,
+                         const size_t panelWidth,
+                         const std::vector<double>& diagonal) {
+    const size_t panelEnd = panelStart + panelWidth;
+    const auto first = std::lower_bound(localRows.begin(), localRows.end(), panelEnd);
+
+    for (auto rowIt = first; rowIt != localRows.end(); ++rowIt) {
+        const size_t localRow = static_cast<size_t>(rowIt - localRows.begin());
+        double* const matrixRow = localMatrix.data() + localRow * n;
+
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < panelWidth; ++column) {
+            double value = matrixRow[panelStart + column];
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
+                value -= matrixRow[panelStart + k] *
+                         diagonal[column * panelWidth + k];
+            }
+            matrixRow[panelStart + column] =
+                value / diagonal[column * panelWidth + column];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void exchangePanelColumns(const std::vector<double>& localMatrix,
+                          const std::vector<size_t>& localRows, const size_t n,
+                          const size_t blockSize, const size_t panelStart,
+                          const size_t panelWidth, const int ranks,
+                          std::vector<double>& panelColumns) {
+    const size_t remainingRows = n - panelStart;
+    const auto first = std::lower_bound(localRows.begin(), localRows.end(), panelStart);
+    const size_t localFirst = static_cast<size_t>(first - localRows.begin());
+    const size_t localPanelRows = localRows.size() - localFirst;
+
+    std::vector<double> localPacked(localPanelRows * panelWidth);
+    for (size_t localRow = localFirst, packedRow = 0; localRow < localRows.size();
+         ++localRow, ++packedRow) {
+        const double* const source = localMatrix.data() + localRow * n + panelStart;
+        std::copy_n(source, panelWidth,
+                    localPacked.data() + packedRow * panelWidth);
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(ranks);
+    size_t packedSize = 0;
+    const size_t blocks = (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < ranks; ++rank) {
+        size_t rowsOnRank = 0;
+        for (size_t block = static_cast<size_t>(rank); block < blocks;
+             block += static_cast<size_t>(ranks)) {
+            const size_t firstRow = block * blockSize;
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(n, firstRow + blockSize);
+            if (lastRow > panelStart) {
+                rowsOnRank += lastRow - std::max(firstRow, panelStart);
+            }
+        }
+        counts[rank] = mpiCount(rowsOnRank * panelWidth);
+        displacements[rank] = mpiCount(packedSize);
+        packedSize += rowsOnRank * panelWidth;
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> packedPanel(packedSize);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(localPacked.data(), mpiCount(localPacked.size()), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                   packedPanel.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                   MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    // Transpose the received panel once.  The trailing update then becomes a
+    // sequence of contiguous SAXPY operations, which is substantially faster
+    // than evaluating a small dot product for every output element.
+    panelColumns.assign(panelWidth * remainingRows, 0.0);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < ranks; ++rank) {
+        size_t packedOffset = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+        for (size_t block = static_cast<size_t>(rank); block < blocks;
+             block += static_cast<size_t>(ranks)) {
+            const size_t firstRow = std::max(block * blockSize, panelStart);
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(n, block * blockSize + blockSize);
+            for (size_t row = firstRow; row < lastRow; ++row) {
+                const double* const source = packedPanel.data() + packedOffset;
+                for (size_t column = 0; column < panelWidth; ++column) {
+                    panelColumns[column * remainingRows + row - panelStart] =
+                        source[column];
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
+                packedOffset += panelWidth;
             }
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+void updateLocalTrailingMatrix(std::vector<double>& localMatrix,
+                               const std::vector<size_t>& localRows,
+                               const size_t n, const size_t panelStart,
+                               const size_t panelWidth,
+                               const std::vector<double>& panelColumns) {
+    const size_t panelEnd = panelStart + panelWidth;
+    const size_t remainingRows = n - panelStart;
+    const auto first = std::lower_bound(localRows.begin(), localRows.end(), panelEnd);
+
+    for (auto rowIt = first; rowIt != localRows.end(); ++rowIt) {
+        const size_t localRow = static_cast<size_t>(rowIt - localRows.begin());
+        const size_t globalRow = *rowIt;
+        double* const matrixRow = localMatrix.data() + localRow * n;
+        const size_t width = globalRow - panelEnd + 1;
+        double* const destination = matrixRow + panelEnd;
+
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < panelWidth; ++column) {
+            const double multiplier = matrixRow[panelStart + column];
+            const double* const source =
+                panelColumns.data() + column * remainingRows + (panelEnd - panelStart);
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+                destination[j] -= multiplier * source[j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+bool distributedCholesky(std::vector<double>& localMatrix,
+                         const std::vector<size_t>& localRows,
+                         const std::vector<size_t>& localIndexByGlobalRow,
+                         const size_t n, const size_t blockSize, const int rank,
+                         const int ranks, size_t& failedDiagonal) {
+    std::vector<double> diagonal;
+    std::vector<double> panelColumns;
+
+    for (size_t panelStart = 0; panelStart < n; panelStart += blockSize) {
+        const size_t panelWidth = std::min(blockSize, n - panelStart);
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>((panelStart / blockSize) %
+                                           static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
+        int success = 1;
+        unsigned long long failedDiagonalWire = 0;
+
+        if (rank == owner &&
+            !factorDiagonalBlock(localMatrix, localIndexByGlobalRow, n, panelStart,
+                                 panelWidth, diagonal, failedDiagonal)) {
+            success = 0;
+        }
+        if (rank == owner) {
+            failedDiagonalWire = static_cast<unsigned long long>(failedDiagonal);
         }
+
+        MPI_Bcast(&success, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        MPI_Bcast(&failedDiagonalWire, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, owner,
+                  MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        failedDiagonal = static_cast<size_t>(failedDiagonalWire);
+        if (!success) {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        if (rank != owner) {
+            diagonal.resize(panelWidth * panelWidth);
+        }
+        MPI_Bcast(diagonal.data(), mpiCount(diagonal.size()), MPI_DOUBLE, owner,
+                  MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        solveLocalPanelRows(localMatrix, localRows, n, panelStart, panelWidth,
+                            diagonal);
+        exchangePanelColumns(localMatrix, localRows, n, blockSize, panelStart,
+                             panelWidth, ranks, panelColumns);
+        updateLocalTrailingMatrix(localMatrix, localRows, n, panelStart, panelWidth,
+                                  panelColumns);
     }
-    
     return true;
 }

    12	
    13	// The matrix is distributed by cyclic row blocks.  Keeping a complete row on
    14	// its owner makes the trailing update contiguous in memory, while cycling the
    15	// blocks balances the progressively larger updates near the bottom of L.
    16	
    17	size_t chooseBlockSize(const size_t n, const int ranks) {
    18	    constexpr size_t minBlockSize = 16;
    19	    constexpr size_t maxBlockSize = 128;
    20	    const size_t target = (n + 4 * static_cast<size_t>(ranks) - 1) /
    21	                          (4 * static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
    22	    return std::min(n, std::clamp(target, minBlockSize, maxBlockSize));
    23	}
    24	
    25	std::vector<size_t> makeLocalRows(const size_t n, const size_t blockSize,
    26	                                  const int rank, const int ranks) {
    27	    std::vector<size_t> rows;
    28	    const size_t blocks = (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize;
    29	
    30	    for (size_t block = static_cast<size_t>(rank); block < blocks;
    31	         block += static_cast<size_t>(ranks)) {
    32	        const size_t first = block * blockSize;
    33	        const size_t last = std::min(n, first + blockSize);
    34	        for (size_t row = first; row < last; ++row) {
    35	            rows.push_back(row);
    36	        }
    37	    }
    38	    return rows;
    39	}
    40	
    41	std::vector<size_t> makeRowsPerRank(const size_t n, const size_t blockSize,
    42	                                    const int ranks) {
    43	    std::vector<size_t> rowCounts(ranks, 0);
    44	    const size_t blocks = (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize;
    45	
    46	    for (size_t block = 0; block < blocks; ++block) {
    47	        const size_t first = block * blockSize;
    48	        rowCounts[block % static_cast<size_t>(ranks)] +=
    49	            std::min(n, first + blockSize) - first;
    50	    }
    51	    return rowCounts;
    52	}
    53	
    54	int mpiCount(const size_t count) {
    55	    // Matrices large enough to exceed an MPI int count require more memory
    56	    // than this benchmark can reasonably allocate on a single rank.
    57	    return static_cast<int>(count);
    58	}
    59	
    60	void broadcastDoubles(std::vector<double>& data, const int root,
    61	                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
    62	    const size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
    63	    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < data.size(); offset += maxCount) {
    64	        const size_t count = std::min(maxCount, data.size() - offset);
    65	        MPI_Bcast(data.data() + offset, mpiCount(count), MPI_DOUBLE, root,
    66	                  communicator);
    67	    }
    68	}
    69	
    70	void generateRandomRows(std::vector<double>& rows, unsigned int& seed) {
    71	    for (double& value : rows) {
    72	        value = (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 0.5;
    73	    }
    74	}

    76	void generateDistributedPositiveDefiniteMatrix(
    77	    std::vector<double>& localMatrix, const std::vector<size_t>& localRows,
    78	    const std::vector<size_t>& localIndexByGlobalRow, const size_t n,
    79	    const size_t blockSize, const int rank) {
    80	    // Retain only the random rows owned by this rank.  Broadcasting the source
    81	    // matrix twice (once to distribute rows and once to form B * B^T) avoids a
    82	    // full n-by-n temporary on every rank and keeps memory scalable.
    83	    std::vector<double> localB(localRows.size() * n);
    84	    std::vector<double> randomRows;
    85	    unsigned int seed = 42;
    86	
    87	    for (size_t firstRow = 0; firstRow < n; firstRow += blockSize) {
    88	        const size_t rowCount = std::min(blockSize, n - firstRow);
    89	        randomRows.resize(rowCount * n);
    90	        if (rank == 0) {
    91	            generateRandomRows(randomRows, seed);
    92	        }
    93	        broadcastDoubles(randomRows, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    94	
    95	        for (size_t row = 0; row < rowCount; ++row) {
    96	            const size_t globalRow = firstRow + row;
    97	            const size_t localRow = localIndexByGlobalRow[globalRow];
    98	            if (localRow != n) {
    99	                std::copy_n(randomRows.data() + row * n, n,
   100	                            localB.data() + localRow * n);
   101	            }
   102	        }
   103	    }
   104	
   105	    std::fill(localMatrix.begin(), localMatrix.end(), 0.0);
   106	    seed = 42;
   107	    for (size_t firstRow = 0; firstRow < n; firstRow += blockSize) {
   108	        const size_t rowCount = std::min(blockSize, n - firstRow);
   109	        randomRows.resize(rowCount * n);
   110	        if (rank == 0) {
   111	            generateRandomRows(randomRows, seed);
   112	        }
   113	        broadcastDoubles(randomRows, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   114	
   115	        for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < localRows.size(); ++localRow) {

   117	            double* const aRow = localMatrix.data() + localRow * n + firstRow;

   119	                const double* const bColumnRow = randomRows.data() + row * n;
   120	                double sum = 0.0;

   286	                panelColumns.data() + column * remainingRows + (panelEnd - panelStart);

   289	            }
   290	        }
   291	    }

   293	
   294	bool distributedCholesky(std::vector<double>& localMatrix,
   295	                         const std::vector<size_t>& localRows,
   296	                         const std::vector<size_t>& localIndexByGlobalRow,
   297	                         const size_t n, const size_t blockSize, const int rank,
   298	                         const int ranks, size_t& failedDiagonal) {
   299	    std::vector<double> diagonal;
   300	    std::vector<double> panelColumns;
   301	
   302	    for (size_t panelStart = 0; panelStart < n; panelStart += blockSize) {
   303	        const size_t panelWidth = std::min(blockSize, n - panelStart);
   304	        const int owner = static_cast<int>((panelStart / blockSize) %
   305	                                           static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
   306	        int success = 1;
   307	        unsigned long long failedDiagonalWire = 0;
   308	
   309	        if (rank == owner &&
   310	            !factorDiagonalBlock(localMatrix, localIndexByGlobalRow, n, panelStart,
   311	                                 panelWidth, diagonal, failedDiagonal)) {
   312	            success = 0;
   313	        }
   314	        if (rank == owner) {
   315	            failedDiagonalWire = static_cast<unsigned long long>(failedDiagonal);
   316	        }
   317	
   318	        MPI_Bcast(&success, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   319	        MPI_Bcast(&failedDiagonalWire, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, owner,
   320	                  MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   321	        failedDiagonal = static_cast<size_t>(failedDiagonalWire);
   322	        if (!success) {
   323	            return false;
   324	        }
   325	
   326	        if (rank != owner) {
   327	            diagonal.resize(panelWidth * panelWidth);
   328	        }
   329	        MPI_Bcast(diagonal.data(), mpiCount(diagonal.size()), MPI_DOUBLE, owner,
   330	                  MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   331	
   332	        solveLocalPanelRows(localMatrix, localRows, n, panelStart, panelWidth,
   333	                            diagonal);
   334	        exchangePanelColumns(localMatrix, localRows, n, blockSize, panelStart,
   335	                             panelWidth, ranks, panelColumns);
   336	        updateLocalTrailingMatrix(localMatrix, localRows, n, panelStart, panelWidth,
   337	                                  panelColumns);
   338	    }
   339	    return true;
   340	}
   341	
   342	void gatherDistributedRows(const std::vector<double>& localMatrix, const size_t n,
   343	                           const size_t blockSize,
   344	                           const std::vector<size_t>& rowsPerRank, const int rank,
   345	                           const int ranks, std::vector<double>& globalMatrix) {
   346	    std::vector<int> counts(ranks);
   347	    std::vector<int> displacements(ranks);
   348	    size_t receivedElements = 0;
   349	    for (int process = 0; process < ranks; ++process) {
   350	        counts[process] = mpiCount(rowsPerRank[process] * n);
   351	        displacements[process] = mpiCount(receivedElements);
   352	        receivedElements += rowsPerRank[process] * n;
   353	    }
   354	
   355	    std::vector<double> packedRows;
   356	    if (rank == 0) {
   357	        packedRows.resize(receivedElements);
   358	    }
   359	    MPI_Gatherv(localMatrix.data(), mpiCount(localMatrix.size()), MPI_DOUBLE,
   360	                packedRows.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
   361	                MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   362	
   363	    if (rank != 0) {
   364	        return;
   365	    }
   366	
   367	    globalMatrix.assign(n * n, 0.0);
   368	    const size_t blocks = (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize;
   369	    for (int process = 0; process < ranks; ++process) {
   370	        size_t packedRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[process]) / n;
   410	    return true;
   411	}
   412	
   413	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   414	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   415	    std::printf("Options:\n");
   416	    std::printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
   417	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   418	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   419	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   420	}
   421	
   422	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   423	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   424	
   425	    int rank = 0;
   426	    int ranks = 1;
   427	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   428	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks);
   429	
   430	    size_t n = 512;
   431	    bool validate = false;
   432	    bool printResults = false;
   433	    bool argumentError = false;
   434	    bool showHelp = false;
   435	
   436	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   437	        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   438	            n = static_cast<size_t>(std::strtoull(argv[++i], nullptr, 10));
   439	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   440	            validate = true;
   441	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   442	            printResults = true;
   443	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   444	            showHelp = true;
   445	            break;
   446	        } else {
   447	            argumentError = true;
   448	            if (rank == 0) {
   449	                std::printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   450	            }
   451	            break;
   452	        }
   453	    }
   454	
   455	    if (showHelp || argumentError || n == 0) {
   456	        if (rank == 0) {
   457	            if (n == 0 && !showHelp && !argumentError) {
   458	                std::printf("Error: Matrix size must be positive\n");
   459	            }
   460	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   461	        }
   462	        MPI_Finalize();
   463	        return argumentError || n == 0 ? 1 : 0;
   464	    }
   465	
   466	    const size_t blockSize = chooseBlockSize(n, ranks);
   467	    const std::vector<size_t> localRows = makeLocalRows(n, blockSize, rank, ranks);
   468	    const std::vector<size_t> rowsPerRank = makeRowsPerRank(n, blockSize, ranks);
   469	    std::vector<size_t> localIndexByGlobalRow(n, n);
   470	    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < localRows.size(); ++localRow) {
   471	        localIndexByGlobalRow[localRows[localRow]] = localRow;
   472	    }
   473	
   474	    if (rank == 0) {
   475	        std::printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
   476	        std::printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
   477	        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   478	        std::printf("MPI ranks: %d\n", ranks);
   479	        std::printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
   480	    }
   481	
   482	    std::vector<double> localMatrix(localRows.size() * n);
   483	    generateDistributedPositiveDefiniteMatrix(
   484	        localMatrix, localRows, localIndexByGlobalRow, n, blockSize, rank);
   485	
   486	    std::vector<double> localOriginal;
   487	    if (validate) {
   488	        localOriginal = localMatrix;
   489	    }
   490	    clearLocalUpperTriangle(localMatrix, localRows, n);
   491	
   492	    if (rank == 0) {
   493	        std::printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
   494	    }
   495	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   496	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   497	    size_t failedDiagonal = 0;
   498	    const bool success = distributedCholesky(
   499	        localMatrix, localRows, localIndexByGlobalRow, n, blockSize, rank, ranks,
   500	        failedDiagonal);
   501	    const double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   502	
   503	    double maximumElapsed = 0.0;
   504	    MPI_Reduce(&elapsed, &maximumElapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
   505	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   506	
   507	    if (!success) {
   508	        if (rank == 0) {
   509	            std::printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
   510	                        failedDiagonal);
   511	            std::printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
   512	        }
   513	        MPI_Finalize();
   514	        return 1;
   515	    }
   516	
   517	    if (rank == 0) {
   518	        const long milliseconds = static_cast<long>(maximumElapsed * 1000.0);
   519	        std::printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", milliseconds);
   520	        const double operations = static_cast<double>(n) * n * n / 3.0;
   521	        const double gflops = operations / maximumElapsed / 1e9;
   522	        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   523	    }
   524	
   525	    std::vector<double> globalMatrix;
   526	    if (printResults || validate) {
   527	        gatherDistributedRows(localMatrix, n, blockSize, rowsPerRank, rank, ranks,
   528	                              globalMatrix);
   529	    }
   530	
   531	    if (printResults && rank == 0) {
   532	        print_results(globalMatrix, "CholeskyL");
   533	    }
   534	
   535	    bool valid = true;
   536	    if (validate) {
   537	        std::vector<double> globalOriginal;
   538	        gatherDistributedRows(localOriginal, n, blockSize, rowsPerRank, rank, ranks,
   539	                              globalOriginal);
   540	        if (rank == 0) {
   541	            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   542	            valid = validateCholesky(globalMatrix, globalOriginal, n);
   543	            std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
   544	        }
   545	        int validationStatus = valid ? 1 : 0;
   546	        MPI_Bcast(&validationStatus, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   547	        valid = validationStatus != 0;
   548	    }
   549	
   550	    MPI_Finalize();

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// The matrix is distributed by cyclic row blocks.  Keeping a complete row on
+// its owner makes the trailing update contiguous in memory, while cycling the
+// blocks balances the progressively larger updates near the bottom of L.
+
+size_t chooseBlockSize(const size_t n, const int ranks) {
+    constexpr size_t minBlockSize = 16;
+    constexpr size_t maxBlockSize = 128;
+    const size_t target = (n + 4 * static_cast<size_t>(ranks) - 1) /
+                          (4 * static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
+    return std::min(n, std::clamp(target, minBlockSize, maxBlockSize));
+}
+
+std::vector<size_t> makeLocalRows(const size_t n, const size_t blockSize,
+                                  const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    std::vector<size_t> rows;
+    const size_t blocks = (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize;
+
+    for (size_t block = static_cast<size_t>(rank); block < blocks;
+         block += static_cast<size_t>(ranks)) {
+        const size_t first = block * blockSize;
+        const size_t last = std::min(n, first + blockSize);
+        for (size_t row = first; row < last; ++row) {
+            rows.push_back(row);
+        }
+    }
+    return rows;
+}
+
+std::vector<size_t> makeRowsPerRank(const size_t n, const size_t blockSize,
+                                    const int ranks) {
+    std::vector<size_t> rowCounts(ranks, 0);
+    const size_t blocks = (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize;
+
+    for (size_t block = 0; block < blocks; ++block) {
+        const size_t first = block * blockSize;
+        rowCounts[block % static_cast<size_t>(ranks)] +=
+            std::min(n, first + blockSize) - first;
+    }
+    return rowCounts;
+}
+
+int mpiCount(const size_t count) {
+    // Matrices large enough to exceed an MPI int count require more memory
+    // than this benchmark can reasonably allocate on a single rank.
+    return static_cast<int>(count);
+}
+
+void broadcastDoubles(std::vector<double>& data, const int root,
+                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < data.size(); offset += maxCount) {
+        const size_t count = std::min(maxCount, data.size() - offset);
+        MPI_Bcast(data.data() + offset, mpiCount(count), MPI_DOUBLE, root,
+                  communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+void generateRandomRows(std::vector<double>& rows, unsigned int& seed) {
+    for (double& value : rows) {
+        value = (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 0.5;
+    }
+}
+
+void generateDistributedPositiveDefiniteMatrix(
+    std::vector<double>& localMatrix, const std::vector<size_t>& localRows,
+    const std::vector<size_t>& localIndexByGlobalRow, const size_t n,
+    const size_t blockSize, const int rank) {
+    // Retain only the random rows owned by this rank.  Broadcasting the source
+    // matrix twice (once to distribute rows and once to form B * B^T) avoids a
+    // full n-by-n temporary on every rank and keeps memory scalable.
+    std::vector<double> localB(localRows.size() * n);
+    std::vector<double> randomRows;
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+
+    for (size_t firstRow = 0; firstRow < n; firstRow += blockSize) {
+        const size_t rowCount = std::min(blockSize, n - firstRow);
+        randomRows.resize(rowCount * n);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            generateRandomRows(randomRows, seed);
+        }
+        broadcastDoubles(randomRows, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+void clearLocalUpperTriangle(std::vector<double>& localMatrix,
+                             const std::vector<size_t>& localRows,
+                             const size_t n) {
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < localRows.size(); ++localRow) {
+        double* const row = localMatrix.data() + localRow * n;
+        std::fill(row + localRows[localRow] + 1, row + n, 0.0);
+    }
+}

+bool factorDiagonalBlock(std::vector<double>& localMatrix,

+void solveLocalPanelRows(std::vector<double>& localMatrix,
+                         const std::vector<size_t>& localRows,
+                         const size_t n, const size_t panelStart,
+                         const size_t panelWidth,
+                         const std::vector<double>& diagonal) {
+    const size_t panelEnd = panelStart + panelWidth;
+    const auto first = std::lower_bound(localRows.begin(), localRows.end(), panelEnd);
+
+    for (auto rowIt = first; rowIt != localRows.end(); ++rowIt) {
+        const size_t localRow = static_cast<size_t>(rowIt - localRows.begin());
+        double* const matrixRow = localMatrix.data() + localRow * n;
+
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < panelWidth; ++column) {
+            double value = matrixRow[panelStart + column];
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
+                value -= matrixRow[panelStart + k] *
+                         diagonal[column * panelWidth + k];
+            }
+            matrixRow[panelStart + column] =
+                value / diagonal[column * panelWidth + column];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void exchangePanelColumns(const std::vector<double>& localMatrix,
+                          const std::vector<size_t>& localRows, const size_t n,
+                          const size_t blockSize, const size_t panelStart,
+                          const size_t panelWidth, const int ranks,
+                          std::vector<double>& panelColumns) {
+    const size_t remainingRows = n - panelStart;
+    const auto first = std::lower_bound(localRows.begin(), localRows.end(), panelStart);
+    const size_t localFirst = static_cast<size_t>(first - localRows.begin());
+    const size_t localPanelRows = localRows.size() - localFirst;
+
+    std::vector<double> localPacked(localPanelRows * panelWidth);
+    for (size_t localRow = localFirst, packedRow = 0; localRow < localRows.size();
+         ++localRow, ++packedRow) {
+        const double* const source = localMatrix.data() + localRow * n + panelStart;
+        std::copy_n(source, panelWidth,
+                    localPacked.data() + packedRow * panelWidth);
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(ranks);
+    size_t packedSize = 0;
+    const size_t blocks = (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < ranks; ++rank) {
+        size_t rowsOnRank = 0;
+        for (size_t block = static_cast<size_t>(rank); block < blocks;
+             block += static_cast<size_t>(ranks)) {
+            const size_t firstRow = block * blockSize;
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(n, firstRow + blockSize);
+            if (lastRow > panelStart) {
+                rowsOnRank += lastRow - std::max(firstRow, panelStart);
+            }
+        }
+        counts[rank] = mpiCount(rowsOnRank * panelWidth);
+        displacements[rank] = mpiCount(packedSize);
+        packedSize += rowsOnRank * panelWidth;
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> packedPanel(packedSize);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(localPacked.data(), mpiCount(localPacked.size()), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                   packedPanel.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                   MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    // Transpose the received panel once.  The trailing update then becomes a
+    // sequence of contiguous SAXPY operations, which is substantially faster
+    // than evaluating a small dot product for every output element.
+    panelColumns.assign(panelWidth * remainingRows, 0.0);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < ranks; ++rank) {
+        size_t packedOffset = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+        for (size_t block = static_cast<size_t>(rank); block < blocks;
+             block += static_cast<size_t>(ranks)) {
+            const size_t firstRow = std::max(block * blockSize, panelStart);
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(n, block * blockSize + blockSize);
+            for (size_t row = firstRow; row < lastRow; ++row) {
+                const double* const source = packedPanel.data() + packedOffset;
+                for (size_t column = 0; column < panelWidth; ++column) {
+                    panelColumns[column * remainingRows + row - panelStart] =
+                        source[column];
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
+                packedOffset += panelWidth;
             }
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+void updateLocalTrailingMatrix(std::vector<double>& localMatrix,
+                               const std::vector<size_t>& localRows,
+                               const size_t n, const size_t panelStart,
+                               const size_t panelWidth,
+                               const std::vector<double>& panelColumns) {
+    const size_t panelEnd = panelStart + panelWidth;
+    const size_t remainingRows = n - panelStart;
+    const auto first = std::lower_bound(localRows.begin(), localRows.end(), panelEnd);
+
+    for (auto rowIt = first; rowIt != localRows.end(); ++rowIt) {
+        const size_t localRow = static_cast<size_t>(rowIt - localRows.begin());
+        const size_t globalRow = *rowIt;
+        double* const matrixRow = localMatrix.data() + localRow * n;
+        const size_t width = globalRow - panelEnd + 1;
+        double* const destination = matrixRow + panelEnd;
+
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < panelWidth; ++column) {
+            const double multiplier = matrixRow[panelStart + column];
+            const double* const source =
+                panelColumns.data() + column * remainingRows + (panelEnd - panelStart);
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+                destination[j] -= multiplier * source[j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+bool distributedCholesky(std::vector<double>& localMatrix,
+                         const std::vector<size_t>& localRows,
+                         const std::vector<size_t>& localIndexByGlobalRow,
+                         const size_t n, const size_t blockSize, const int rank,
+                         const int ranks, size_t& failedDiagonal) {
+    std::vector<double> diagonal;
+    std::vector<double> panelColumns;
+
+    for (size_t panelStart = 0; panelStart < n; panelStart += blockSize) {
+        const size_t panelWidth = std::min(blockSize, n - panelStart);
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>((panelStart / blockSize) %
+                                           static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
+        int success = 1;
+        unsigned long long failedDiagonalWire = 0;
+
+        if (rank == owner &&
+            !factorDiagonalBlock(localMatrix, localIndexByGlobalRow, n, panelStart,
+                                 panelWidth, diagonal, failedDiagonal)) {
+            success = 0;
+        }
+        if (rank == owner) {
+            failedDiagonalWire = static_cast<unsigned long long>(failedDiagonal);
         }
+
+        MPI_Bcast(&success, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        MPI_Bcast(&failedDiagonalWire, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, owner,
+                  MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        failedDiagonal = static_cast<size_t>(failedDiagonalWire);
+        if (!success) {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        if (rank != owner) {
+            diagonal.resize(panelWidth * panelWidth);
+        }
+        MPI_Bcast(diagonal.data(), mpiCount(diagonal.size()), MPI_DOUBLE, owner,
+                  MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        solveLocalPanelRows(localMatrix, localRows, n, panelStart, panelWidth,
+                            diagonal);
+        exchangePanelColumns(localMatrix, localRows, n, blockSize, panelStart,
+                             panelWidth, ranks, panelColumns);
+        updateLocalTrailingMatrix(localMatrix, localRows, n, panelStart, panelWidth,
+                                  panelColumns);
     }
-    
     return true;

Implemented MPI-distributed Cholesky in [cholesky.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/cholesky_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_mpi_r3/cholesky/cholesky.cpp:294).

- Block-cyclic row distribution balances trailing updates across MPI ranks.
- Blocked panel factorization uses broadcasts and collective panel exchange.
- SPD generation is distributed and memory-scalable.
- Output and validation remain root-only with the original executable name/options.

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// The matrix is distributed by cyclic row blocks.  Keeping a complete row on
+// its owner makes the trailing update contiguous in memory, while cycling the
+// blocks balances the progressively larger updates near the bottom of L.
+
+size_t chooseBlockSize(const size_t n, const int ranks) {
+    constexpr size_t minBlockSize = 16;
+    constexpr size_t maxBlockSize = 128;
+    const size_t target = (n + 4 * static_cast<size_t>(ranks) - 1) /
+                          (4 * static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
+    return std::min(n, std::clamp(target, minBlockSize, maxBlockSize));
+}
+
+std::vector<size_t> makeLocalRows(const size_t n, const size_t blockSize,
+                                  const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    std::vector<size_t> rows;
+    const size_t blocks = (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize;
+
+    for (size_t block = static_cast<size_t>(rank); block < blocks;
+         block += static_cast<size_t>(ranks)) {
+        const size_t first = block * blockSize;
+        const size_t last = std::min(n, first + blockSize);
+        for (size_t row = first; row < last; ++row) {
+            rows.push_back(row);
+        }
+    }
+    return rows;
+}
+
+std::vector<size_t> makeRowsPerRank(const size_t n, const size_t blockSize,
+                                    const int ranks) {
+    std::vector<size_t> rowCounts(ranks, 0);
+    const size_t blocks = (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize;
+
+    for (size_t block = 0; block < blocks; ++block) {
+        const size_t first = block * blockSize;
+        rowCounts[block % static_cast<size_t>(ranks)] +=
+            std::min(n, first + blockSize) - first;
+    }
+    return rowCounts;
+}
+
+int mpiCount(const size_t count) {
+    // Matrices large enough to exceed an MPI int count require more memory
+    // than this benchmark can reasonably allocate on a single rank.
+    return static_cast<int>(count);
+}
+
+void broadcastDoubles(std::vector<double>& data, const int root,
+                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < data.size(); offset += maxCount) {
+        const size_t count = std::min(maxCount, data.size() - offset);
+        MPI_Bcast(data.data() + offset, mpiCount(count), MPI_DOUBLE, root,
+                  communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+void generateRandomRows(std::vector<double>& rows, unsigned int& seed) {
+    for (double& value : rows) {
+        value = (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 0.5;
+    }
+}
+
+void generateDistributedPositiveDefiniteMatrix(
+    std::vector<double>& localMatrix, const std::vector<size_t>& localRows,
+    const std::vector<size_t>& localIndexByGlobalRow, const size_t n,
+    const size_t blockSize, const int rank) {
+    // Retain only the random rows owned by this rank.  Broadcasting the source
+    // matrix twice (once to distribute rows and once to form B * B^T) avoids a
+    // full n-by-n temporary on every rank and keeps memory scalable.
+    std::vector<double> localB(localRows.size() * n);
+    std::vector<double> randomRows;
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+
+    for (size_t firstRow = 0; firstRow < n; firstRow += blockSize) {
+        const size_t rowCount = std::min(blockSize, n - firstRow);
+        randomRows.resize(rowCount * n);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            generateRandomRows(randomRows, seed);
+        }
+        broadcastDoubles(randomRows, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
 
-bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
-    // A is stored in row-major order

+            const size_t localRow = localIndexByGlobalRow[globalRow];
+            if (localRow != n) {
+                std::copy_n(randomRows.data() + row * n, n,
+                            localB.data() + localRow * n);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    std::fill(localMatrix.begin(), localMatrix.end(), 0.0);
+    seed = 42;
+    for (size_t firstRow = 0; firstRow < n; firstRow += blockSize) {
+        const size_t rowCount = std::min(blockSize, n - firstRow);
+        randomRows.resize(rowCount * n);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            generateRandomRows(randomRows, seed);
+        }
+        broadcastDoubles(randomRows, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < localRows.size(); ++localRow) {
+            const double* const bRow = localB.data() + localRow * n;
+            double* const aRow = localMatrix.data() + localRow * n + firstRow;
+            for (size_t row = 0; row < rowCount; ++row) {
+                const double* const bColumnRow = randomRows.data() + row * n;
+                double sum = 0.0;

+                    sum += bRow[k] * bColumnRow[k];

+                aRow[row] = sum;
+            }
+        }
+    }

+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < localRows.size(); ++localRow) {
+        localMatrix[localRow * n + localRows[localRow]] +=

+}

+void clearLocalUpperTriangle(std::vector<double>& localMatrix,
+                             const std::vector<size_t>& localRows,
+                             const size_t n) {
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < localRows.size(); ++localRow) {
+        double* const row = localMatrix.data() + localRow * n;
+        std::fill(row + localRows[localRow] + 1, row + n, 0.0);
+    }
+}

+bool factorDiagonalBlock(std::vector<double>& localMatrix,
+                         const std::vector<size_t>& localIndexByGlobalRow,
+                         const size_t n, const size_t panelStart,

+
+void solveLocalPanelRows(std::vector<double>& localMatrix,
+                         const std::vector<size_t>& localRows,
+                         const size_t n, const size_t panelStart,
+                         const size_t panelWidth,
+                         const std::vector<double>& diagonal) {
+    const size_t panelEnd = panelStart + panelWidth;
+    const auto first = std::lower_bound(localRows.begin(), localRows.end(), panelEnd);
+
+    for (auto rowIt = first; rowIt != localRows.end(); ++rowIt) {
+        const size_t localRow = static_cast<size_t>(rowIt - localRows.begin());
+        double* const matrixRow = localMatrix.data() + localRow * n;
+
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < panelWidth; ++column) {
+            double value = matrixRow[panelStart + column];
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
+                value -= matrixRow[panelStart + k] *
+                         diagonal[column * panelWidth + k];
+            }
+            matrixRow[panelStart + column] =
+                value / diagonal[column * panelWidth + column];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void exchangePanelColumns(const std::vector<double>& localMatrix,
+                          const std::vector<size_t>& localRows, const size_t n,
+                          const size_t blockSize, const size_t panelStart,
+                          const size_t panelWidth, const int ranks,
+                          std::vector<double>& panelColumns) {
+    const size_t remainingRows = n - panelStart;
+    const auto first = std::lower_bound(localRows.begin(), localRows.end(), panelStart);
+    const size_t localFirst = static_cast<size_t>(first - localRows.begin());
+    const size_t localPanelRows = localRows.size() - localFirst;
+
+    std::vector<double> localPacked(localPanelRows * panelWidth);
+    for (size_t localRow = localFirst, packedRow = 0; localRow < localRows.size();
+         ++localRow, ++packedRow) {
+        const double* const source = localMatrix.data() + localRow * n + panelStart;
+        std::copy_n(source, panelWidth,
+                    localPacked.data() + packedRow * panelWidth);
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(ranks);
+    size_t packedSize = 0;
+    const size_t blocks = (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < ranks; ++rank) {
+        size_t rowsOnRank = 0;
+        for (size_t block = static_cast<size_t>(rank); block < blocks;
+             block += static_cast<size_t>(ranks)) {
+            const size_t firstRow = block * blockSize;
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(n, firstRow + blockSize);
+            if (lastRow > panelStart) {
+                rowsOnRank += lastRow - std::max(firstRow, panelStart);
+            }
+        }
+        counts[rank] = mpiCount(rowsOnRank * panelWidth);
+        displacements[rank] = mpiCount(packedSize);
+        packedSize += rowsOnRank * panelWidth;
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> packedPanel(packedSize);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(localPacked.data(), mpiCount(localPacked.size()), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                   packedPanel.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                   MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    // Transpose the received panel once.  The trailing update then becomes a
+    // sequence of contiguous SAXPY operations, which is substantially faster
+    // than evaluating a small dot product for every output element.
+    panelColumns.assign(panelWidth * remainingRows, 0.0);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < ranks; ++rank) {
+        size_t packedOffset = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+        for (size_t block = static_cast<size_t>(rank); block < blocks;
+             block += static_cast<size_t>(ranks)) {
+            const size_t firstRow = std::max(block * blockSize, panelStart);
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(n, block * blockSize + blockSize);
+            for (size_t row = firstRow; row < lastRow; ++row) {
+                const double* const source = packedPanel.data() + packedOffset;
+                for (size_t column = 0; column < panelWidth; ++column) {
+                    panelColumns[column * remainingRows + row - panelStart] =
+                        source[column];
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
+                packedOffset += panelWidth;
             }
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+void updateLocalTrailingMatrix(std::vector<double>& localMatrix,
+                               const std::vector<size_t>& localRows,
+                               const size_t n, const size_t panelStart,
+                               const size_t panelWidth,
+                               const std::vector<double>& panelColumns) {
+    const size_t panelEnd = panelStart + panelWidth;
+    const size_t remainingRows = n - panelStart;
+    const auto first = std::lower_bound(localRows.begin(), localRows.end(), panelEnd);
+
+    for (auto rowIt = first; rowIt != localRows.end(); ++rowIt) {
+        const size_t localRow = static_cast<size_t>(rowIt - localRows.begin());
+        const size_t globalRow = *rowIt;
+        double* const matrixRow = localMatrix.data() + localRow * n;
+        const size_t width = globalRow - panelEnd + 1;
+        double* const destination = matrixRow + panelEnd;
+
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < panelWidth; ++column) {
+            const double multiplier = matrixRow[panelStart + column];
+            const double* const source =
+                panelColumns.data() + column * remainingRows + (panelEnd - panelStart);
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+                destination[j] -= multiplier * source[j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+bool distributedCholesky(std::vector<double>& localMatrix,
+                         const std::vector<size_t>& localRows,
+                         const std::vector<size_t>& localIndexByGlobalRow,
+                         const size_t n, const size_t blockSize, const int rank,
+                         const int ranks, size_t& failedDiagonal) {
+    std::vector<double> diagonal;
+    std::vector<double> panelColumns;
+
+    for (size_t panelStart = 0; panelStart < n; panelStart += blockSize) {
+        const size_t panelWidth = std::min(blockSize, n - panelStart);
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>((panelStart / blockSize) %
+                                           static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
+        int success = 1;
+        unsigned long long failedDiagonalWire = 0;
+
+        if (rank == owner &&
+            !factorDiagonalBlock(localMatrix, localIndexByGlobalRow, n, panelStart,
+                                 panelWidth, diagonal, failedDiagonal)) {
+            success = 0;
+        }
+        if (rank == owner) {
+            failedDiagonalWire = static_cast<unsigned long long>(failedDiagonal);
         }
+
+        MPI_Bcast(&success, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        MPI_Bcast(&failedDiagonalWire, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, owner,
+                  MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        failedDiagonal = static_cast<size_t>(failedDiagonalWire);
+        if (!success) {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        if (rank != owner) {
+            diagonal.resize(panelWidth * panelWidth);
+        }
+        MPI_Bcast(diagonal.data(), mpiCount(diagonal.size()), MPI_DOUBLE, owner,
+                  MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        solveLocalPanelRows(localMatrix, localRows, n, panelStart, panelWidth,
+                            diagonal);
+        exchangePanelColumns(localMatrix, localRows, n, blockSize, panelStart,
+                             panelWidth, ranks, panelColumns);
+        updateLocalTrailingMatrix(localMatrix, localRows, n, panelStart, panelWidth,
+                                  panelColumns);
     }
-    
     return true;
 }
